CURRENT VALIDATION - 2026-10-28
2026-10-28 Don't Trip2216 production binding and shared party-defense shouts

Don't Trip is a paid instant PvE shout:5Energy,20s recharge,no cast or aftercast.
Earned Deldrimor rank0/1 gives3seconds,rank2/3 gives4seconds,and rank4..10 gives
5seconds. Current living party members within1000 units gain timed knockdown
immunity. Registered NPC companions require scoped party membership. A dead
party owner's living companion remains a member. Nearby nonparty allies do
not receive the shout. Moving away or leaving the party does not remove an
already accepted application. Death,generation replacement and natural expiry
retire the owned status;renewal replaces only the same skill.

Don't Trip and Shields Up now share a typed World transaction and NPC recipient
primitive. Public Shields Up entrypoints remain compatible. Canonical plans
and recipient entries remain skill-specific;Shields Up retains its projectile
armor consumer,while Don't Trip feeds both player and NPC knockdown authority.
Intrinsic unknown NPC knockdown policy remains unknown after the shout expires;
active reviewed immunity prevents knockdown without inventing that metadata.
NPC expiry publication tracks recipient lifetime plus skill,so coexisting
shouts cannot erase each other's monitor. Linked resource/expiry events settle
before copied registries are retained,avoiding stale-state restoration.

The archived Shout page explicitly allows use while knocked down or while
another skill is activating. The shared World producer no longer rejects a
knocked-down caster. Applying a shout does not stand the caster up. Runtime
registers2216 as an instant action and preserves an in-progress spell. Initial
paid tests caught missing instant registration and selected-target normalization;
these were corrected before publication. Untargeted party heals now likewise
normalize to the caster,including Breath2221 when a foe is selected.

129 new native cases PASS:66 rank/range/party combinations,33 NPC intrinsic-
policy/action-preservation combinations,coexistence/renewal,dead-owner membership,
2 knocked-down shared-shout cases,stale/forged scope/plan rejection,24 normal
paid Runtime/missing-hook/Energy/PvP/scope cases,and an ongoing-spell preservation
case. The278 Breath cases also pass with a foe selected for the untargeted cast.
Python mutation guards retain shared NPC status allocation/expiry checks and
verify both immunity consumers,instant registration and producer routing.

Fixed source evidence:two local map280 instant activations in
20261024-all-completed/packets-19472-19186421.jsonl. Lines551509/551510/551512/
551513 and551737/551738/551740/551741 show48 activation,caster body1901,
5second2216 status and slot publication. The original2022 instant handler
produces no timed cast event;caster1901 resolves assetd39a04010000. No58 spell
terminal or ordinary cast animation is invented. The archived wiki oldid2666149
supplies the duration progression. Synthetic actor/model/asset services do not
prove rendered acceptance. Self receipts do not prove party radius,rank or
actual control prevention. Native metadata uses1000 range;the existing Shields
Up1012 policy is retained pending controlled retail geometry/layer measurement.

Fixed inventory:679 captured baseline skills,404 source-bound,275 awaiting
binding and650 missing local activation captures. Binding is a source claim,
not complete gameplay or visual acceptance. The fixed manifest and manual build
queue remain unchanged. Official clients and loggers were not accessed.

Individual temporary-NPC retirement now erases the exact retired party member
and its party-defense status monitors,so despawns cannot retain stale bookkeeping.
Both natural expiry and explicit retirement are protected by mutation checks.

Final validation:7 current native suites and42 Python checks PASS. The final
Release host build and --build-info exit0;the corrected membership regression
was rebuilt separately and passes. An earlier expectation that death dissolved
a living companion's membership was replaced with checks for renewal on owner
death,no further renewal after owner departure,and exactly one renewed expiry.

and the final rebuilt test executables support the current validation claim.

2026-10-21 active equipment and typed duration integration

Weapon-only calculation uses whole-second half-up rounding and the documented1second minimum. Fractional base durations with a nonzero bonus,overflow and active Blessed Aura256are rejected instead of inventing stacking. No-bonus durations preserve their exact base. Receipts cannot be reused at a later timestamp or for another skill/caster.

Shared World regeneration/protective application preserves immutable native plans but installs adjusted expiry in both effect registry and regeneration source. Staged Watchful extends player and NPC recipient timers;its early-end reward checks the actual adjusted expiry. NPC recipient primitive receives a trusted duration only after World validates the receipt. Later equipment changes do not rewrite an installed deadline.

Gateway completion callbacks now derive quotes from that session's current inventory revision,validated active weapon set and current owned effect snapshot. The existing protective/regeneration enchantment paths consume these quotes;non-enchantment Troll Unguent446retains its native duration. Actual Watchful1392callback remains empty for the separate remaining fidelity/rendered gates.

New enchantment_duration suite covers active/inactive/physical-shadow/two-handed source selection,invalid references/types/quantity/flags/duplicates,time/scope/skill binding,minimum1second,rounding,fractional/overflow guards and unsupported Aura stacking. World cases verify Watchful12second player/NPC regeneration,12second wire timer,late removal inside the added interval,nonretroactive equipment changes,Healing Breeze15to18second regeneration and Blood Renewal7to8second expiry/end healing. These are authority/timing tests,not rendered acceptance or universal stacking proof.

2026-10-21 Shield of Judgment contact journal

Added gw_shield_judgment_contacts.h,a staged instance-owned release/qualification/terminal journal for exact defender lifetimes. It snapshots an owned reaction only at qualified contact and consumes that proposal before damage/control callbacks. Duplicate terminal events cannot replay it;cancelled/retired/malformed terminal facts retire or reject according to exact ownership. It does not call World or publish packets yet.

Release IDs are validated at release,not impact. This is necessary because ranged projectiles can land out of release order. Per-source high-water release IDs reject replay without rejecting a legitimate older projectile arriving after a newer one. The4096contact bound covers outstanding releases,not lifetime total attacks;consumed contacts free capacity. 2026-10-22 World bundle transaction stage:

World now owns transient pickup separately from durable bag credit. Pickup consumes

one exact floor lifetime, retains its item definition, cancels ordinary attack and

NPC interaction, and assigns opcode336 to its owner only. Generic inventory credit

rejects type6 with held_bundle_path_required; no unreviewed policy is inferred.

Drop prepares a copy with ground-revision/allocation-cursor/actor-lifetime checks.

Persistent drops allocate fresh wire and actor lifetimes and retain metadata;

destroy disposition publishes deletion without respawning. Manual drop requires

the exact held generation, rejecting delayed requests against a replacement.

Recall and Death's Charge prepare/commit this transition at the origin before

position changes. Recall owner publication includes clear/drop before trails and

retained disable/removal/position feedback; peers receive shared floor packets.

Ordinary attack entry and weapon-skill completion reject held bundles. This does

not yet prove every attack admission, stat suppression or peer equipment update.

stale manual drop, nonmutating Recall preview, persistent/destroy return, fresh

identities and origin placement. First three suites passed; expanded rebuild and

regression pending in build/reports/bundle-world-build.txt.

2026-10-22 persistent bundle floor-capacity ownership:

A persistent held bundle now retains one logical floor-capacity lease. Both World

floor producers (durable inventory drop and NPC loot) check physical floor entries

plus held persistent reservations before accepting another item. Pickup trades one

floor entry for one held reservation; release trades the reservation back for a

floor entry. Destroy-on-drop bundles reserve no slot. Multiple holders each reserve

one. The stored original item/held generation remains the sole release authority.

The previously reproduced full-pool owned Recall exception is prevented for this

resource:63 ordinary floor entries plus one held persistent bundle is effective

capacity64. Further inventory drop rejects BEFORE its durable debit callback.

Maintenance cancellation can then drop at origin and return, consuming the reserved

slot without waiting for unrelated loot to leave. This is not a delayed/backdated

Recall retry, and no fabricated later movement timestamp is introduced.

The earlier direct-component full64-floor rejection test is superseded: such a

physical occupancy is now denied while a persistent bundle is held. Tests exercise

owned cancellation at effective capacity plus multi-holder and destroy policies.

Identity exhaustion, navigation failure and publication-resource failures remain

separate staged end-transaction gaps. Held departure/death/weapon-switch lifecycle

cleanup and effects are still unimplemented; a retained departed holder can reserve

capacity until cleanup is implemented. Client bundle requests and Recall925 stay

unbound. This change does not claim a general automatic retry solution.



2026-10-22 bundle death/departure lifecycle stage:

Local Bundle.html revision2627020 explicitly lists involuntary drops after dying

and entering another zone. World now snapshots the authoritative origin before

death resets movement and before departure erases the actor. Death publication

commits the bundle release after setting corpse health; departure commits before

removing the old lifetime. Ground/destroy dispositions retain their explicit

content policy. A released item gets fresh floor identity at the captured origin.

Release work is retained by exact owner scope,held generation,cause and origin.

If allocation fails, death/departure can finish and the original held metadata

and capacity reservation survive for a later tick attempt. An absent/reused owner

receives no old owner336clear; peer definition deletion/floor publication remain

shared. Successful orphan completion removes old bundle state; successful normal

departure clears that state immediately. Release generation prevents a replacement

actor from inheriting old held authority. No item-spell effects are invented.

New tests cover death/departure with both dispositions,once-only definition removal,

no repeated release on subsequent tick/death,origin preservation and actor-ID reuse.

Pending allocation-failure isolation/authority tests and weapon-switch/hold-bonus/

peer-equipment/native visual proof remain; normal bundle and925requests stay gated.

This lifecycle queue does NOT solve the separate owned Recall navigation/publication

failure transaction. No official process or live capture was touched.



2026-10-22 weapon switching and pending-release isolation:

World switch_weapon_set now validates exact owner/optional held generation,clock

and absence of a pending lifecycle release,prepares the origin drop,and invokes a

trusted durable inventory CAS only after preparation succeeds. Failed CAS leaves

held/floor state unchanged and emits no drop. Successful CAS commits the prepared

release once under the same World lock. Callback must not reenter World.

The 37130 opcode48 gateway now uses this transaction around its existing inventory

projection/save/commit; unchanged active-set selection retains its previous no-op

behavior. Source refresh of weapon stats is not misclassified as a switch.

Ordinary manual/shadow drop commit rejects a lifecycle-pending holder,so it cannot

steal a death/departure release and leave that event orphaned. The lifecycle

consumer alone can release its captured generation/origin.

Expanded bundle_world tests verify stale/missing held generation prevents durable

callbacks,failed save produces no drop,successful switch clears first and applies

ground/destroy policy,then ordinary no-bundle switching remains usable.

An actual allocator-exhaustion test reserves the remaining monotonic floor wire

IDs after pickup. Death no longer throws:original held metadata survives,persistent

capacity remains reserved,and subsequent tick attempts do not fabricate a floor

identity or delete the original definition. After resurrection,switch CAS is not

called while release remains pending. Departure/actor-ID reuse yields no stale

owner336clear. This is failure retention/isolation proof,NOT allocator recovery:

this monotonic pool cannot recover exhausted IDs without a new World instance.

Still gated:held weapon/stat suppression,authored item-spell effects,expiry/renewal,

peer equipment and rendered/native drop sequencing. Owned Recall navigation or

publication failure remains a distinct unresolved end-transaction concern.

No official client or capture changed;normal bundle pickup and925 remain disabled.
